# Secondmate registry vs filesystem audit (2026-10-15)

## Outcome

The filesystem confirms **1 of 7 registered homes usable now**: `system-ops`. Its exact registered directory exists, has all four requested home markers, and has fresh state-file activity. The other six registered paths are absent. I found same-number clone/worktree candidates elsewhere, but none contains `data/secondmates.md` or `data/captain.md`; they cannot be identified as the missing secondmates from filesystem evidence alone. No home or registry was changed.

## Method and limits

Read-only inspection only. I read `/home/hd2admin/kun-agent-workspace/data/secondmates.md` (entries at lines 1-2 and 4-8; line 3 is blank), tested each registered path, checked `state/`, `data/`, `bin/`, and `AGENTS.md`, inspected candidate self-description files if present, and measured newest file mtimes. The home registry and captain files were both absent in the extant `system-ops` home and in all six same-number candidate clones inspected. The registered home path itself and its current activity are stronger filesystem facts than a matching clone name.

The pinned baseline policy pointer `/home/hd2admin/AGENT-POLICY.md` and boot board pointers `/root/agent-work/deightonllm-firstmate/{CONTROLLER_STATE.md,BOARD.md}` were not present at those exact paths (read attempts returned ENOENT); this did not change the read-only evidence scope. The account policy `/home/hd2admin/agent-work/AGENTS.md`, routing and bounded-execution pointers, lean-ctx guidance, and warehouse index were readable. No tasks-axi command was run.

## Per-secondmate findings and usable capacity

- **system-ops — LIVE AND REGISTERED CORRECTLY.** This is the sole usable registered home on the inspected filesystem. The markers exist and its newest file mtime is 08:18:31 on 2026-10-15. The registry scopes it to explicit parent-assigned Firstmate system/agent-infrastructure tickets; it must remain idle absent such a ticket. This is the one assignment it could accept, within that scope.
- **elevate-p0-repair — GONE.** Registered path absent. This is not evidence that it is the Elevate secondmate.

Confirmation would require independent identity evidence from the candidate itself or its authoritative provisioning/launch records; a same-number path, Firstmate repo contents, and markers are insufficient.

## Recommendation

Do not rewrite the registry by substituting same-number clones based on path resemblance. Keep the current list as the record of claimed assignments, treat only `system-ops` as usable on this filesystem, and obtain independent home-identity/provisioning evidence for each candidate before any future registration repair. This scout made no such repair.

## Completion-gate limitation

The required `decision-hold-lifecycle` skill was read. This report surfaces no unresolved choice requiring a captain decision; the candidate identities remain unproven, so the recommendation is to confirm identity from an authoritative provisioning/launch record before changing registration. I did not run `bin/fm-decision-hold.sh complete --none`: its implementation checks `tasks-axi` compatibility and writes the originating task's metadata (and may append to its status file), while the brief explicitly forbids running tasks-axi and permits writes outside this worktree only to this report and the named status file. I did not redirect the gate to another home or bypass it. Therefore the report artifact is written, but the shared completion gate remains **unverified** and needs the owning Firstmate to complete it via an allowed path.
